# Validation report

Written by `photonoxide validate`; don't edit it by hand. CI fails when a case fails or this file is out of date. Values have six significant digits; a value that should be zero shows as "≤ tolerance" when it is within it.

Every tolerance follows from a rule (#544): the case's **measure**, relative (rel) or in its own unit (abs), and its **basis**: round-off for an exact discrete identity (at most 1e-8 relative); discretisation for a closed form against our grid, whose order another case checks; reference for a published number, its stated uncertainty or half a unit of its last printed digit; model for a published approximation, the accuracy its paper gives it; order for a convergence test, the scheme's order within a margin. **Error** is the case's error in that measure, to two digits (or under 1 % of the tolerance). **Ours** is our own numerical error beside it: exact (no grid to converge), what the case itself measures, or a grid study's estimate, which must be under a third of the tolerance. A case whose rule isn't written yet says so.
